Textise icon
Textise
 1 like

Textise is a new way of looking at the Web. It’s an internet tool that removes everything from a web page except for its text. In practice, this means that images, forms, scripts, adverts, they all go, leaving plain text.
Cost / License

    Free
    Proprietary 

Platforms

    Online

AlternativeTo is a free service that helps you find better alternatives to the products you love and hate.

The site is made by Ola and Markus in Sweden, with a lot of help from our friends and colleagues in Italy, Finland, USA, Colombia, Philippines, France and contributors from all over the world. That's right, all the lists of alternatives are crowd-sourced, and that's what makes the data powerful and relevant.

Feel free to send us your questions and feedback at hello@alternativeto.net, on our Discord or on our social media channels X, Mastodon, Bluesky, Threads, Instagram, or LinkedIn. If you like AlternativeTo, please consider supporting us using Liberapay or PayPal.
